## Summary

Lycosantalonol retains matching C20 stored forms with five unresolved tetrahedral centres, a source-only tomato producer/locus assertion, and two carefully bounded historical mechanism graphs. The computed Santalane sesquiterpenoid classification remains a model applicability concern. The accepted original maintained result preserves the complete owner and both historical graphs within their stated verification limits.

## Scientific And Domain Assessments

### Identity

identity: unknown. Targets: naturalproductmech:mibig-7163f2db37.

The minted identifier naturalproductmech:mibig-7163f2db37 and complete key QSUSRIKETCUEAR-RHAKXUACSA-N retain MIBiG BGC0001322 version 3 lycosantalonol. PubChem 101900499 is not freshly resolved; the saved ChEBI exact-structure query is empty within its stated scope. The retained partial stereochemical form and the historical paper’s assigned transgenic-product identity are not independently elevated to a uniquely established natural isolate. The β-phellandrene companion shares a locus but has a distinct complete key and a different compound-evidence citation.

### Representation

representation: supported. Targets: naturalproductmech:mibig-7163f2db37.

Both complete forms are neutral C20H32O2 with 22 heavy atoms, 24 bonds and one component. They retain a bridged carbocyclic core containing a three-membered ring, a side-chain ketone, a tertiary hydroxyl and an alkene with identical methyl substituents at one end, so that alkene has no E/Z requirement. One tetrahedral centre is specified R and five potential tetrahedral centres remain unspecified, matching stereo_complete:false. The saved ring lists contain alternative cycles rather than four independent rings. All ten original comparison flags are true, and the recorded 22-atom correspondence remains explicitly short of independent graph/natural-identity proof. No parser, structure cleanup or experimental stereo assignment was added.

### Origin

provenance: supported. Targets: naturalproductmech:mibig-7163f2db37.

MIBiG BGC0001322 version 3 retains Solanum lycopersicum (NCBITaxon:4081), GenBank KC807995.1, TERPENE class and active status. Producer SOURCE_ASSERTION and locus CLUSTER_UNSTATED remain distinct; raw source quality is questionable and completeness unknown. Neither later curator history nor engineered-tissue observations changes those source grades, establishes a wild-type producer strain or fills the source-only locus method. β-Phellandrene’s compound claim cannot be transferred solely because it shares BGC0001322.

### Citations

evidence: supported. Targets: naturalproductmech:mibig-7163f2db37.

The source producer/locus citation DOI:10.1371/journal.pone.0119302 is retained at COMPOUND_EVIDENCE level as DATABASE_ASSERTION. The two curated graphs separately retain historical PRIMARY_EXPERIMENT, DATABASE_ASSERTION and CURATOR_INFERENCE placements with precise scope. Their notes report prior main-paper, Figures2–3, S1 and S2 inspection while retaining other unread supplementary and earlier-paper limits. These are historical qualified assertions, not newly accessed publications. β-Phellandrene’s distinct DOI:10.1073/pnas.0904113106 stays with its own compound context.

### Activity

evidence: supported. Targets: naturalproductmech:mibig-7163f2db37.

No occurrence list, bioactivity observation or pharmacological molecular-target root is declared for this complete owner. The retained PubChem CID101900499 row has a blank AID and a no-assay-data sentinel, which is not an inactive measurement. Exact empty occurrence, BindingDB and sibling-corpus joins are bounded by their stored predicates. The companion β-phellandrene’s 341 raw LOTUS placements and its saved 73828→3024264 taxon merge are context only; they supply no lycosantalonol occurrence or producer upgrade. Biosynthetic protein examples in the graphs are not pharmacological target evidence.

### Classification

consistency: concern. Targets: naturalproductmech:mibig-7163f2db37.

NPClassifier npclassifier.gnps2.org@2026-09-07 faithfully retains Terpenoids, Sesquiterpenoids, Santalane sesquiterpenoids and is_glycoside:false. The complete C20 oxygenated representation and the retained C20 precursor narrative make the sesquiterpenoid label an applicability concern, while the santalane-like core alone does not settle whole-molecule biosynthetic classification. No wrong source identity or directory change follows automatically from a faithful model prediction. The full tables contain no sugar residue or O-carbon-O linkage that would contradict the false glycoside output.

### Pathway

graph: supported. Targets: naturalproductmech:mibig-7163f2db37.

The complete raw owner declares two BIOSYNTHESIS graphs and no biosynthetic_pathway root. The CPT2 assay/transgenic graph has eleven nodes and ten individually supported edges; the transcript-context graph has ten nodes and twelve edges. The first separates precursor condensation from engineered-tissue product detection. Recombinant CPT2 lacking 51 residues was assayed with IPP and Z,Z-FPP using a hydrolyzed radiolabel readout assigned by the source to NNPP, not direct final-product catalysis or independent precursor structural proof. The selected three 35S MP1 plants and transgenic petiolule comparisons retain source assignment, bounded wild-type nondetection and the unresolved S2 peak4 dominant-ion difference between chromatogramD and B/C/standardE. The second graph preserves normalized qRT-PCR tissue/fraction patterns, not single-cell localization, coordinated protein abundance, direct regulation, physical assembly or reactions imported from incompletely inspected earlier papers. The accepted original maintained result preserves the complete owner, both graphs, their ordered evidence and all four history events; the biosynthetic_pathway root remains absent. This establishes preservation, not independent biochemical verification.

### Protein

graph: supported. Targets: naturalproductmech:mibig-7163f2db37.

K7WQ45, R9R6P6 and K4CI56 are deliberate current native-sequence examples for CPT2, TPS21 and CYP71BN1, not proven historical assay stocks or MP1 alleles. The CPT2 preparation and 35S construct remain REVIEW_NEEDED and ungrounded; the 51-residue deletion differs from the 1–61 predicted transit region and does not establish mature processing or tag handling. The retained CPT2 CDS comparisons and all three S1 primer matches were bounded to the eleven annotated KC807995.1 CDSs, not genome-wide specificity. K4CI56 retains its Heinz1706 source and generic P450 scope. Transcript support does not demonstrate purified TPS21/CYP71BN1 reaction chemistry, complexes or direct final-product catalysis. Historical sequence checks are carried with their limits and are not refreshed.

## Limits And Additional Notes

- All evidence is retained locally. No external publication, identifier, taxonomy, protein sequence or natural stereochemical assertion was refreshed under the absolute no-request instruction.
- Full QC and complete OAK authority coverage remain unavailable. Original gates, stored-form parsing and maintained-reader preservation establish only their stated scope.
- Exact empty joins are bounded by saved predicates/snapshots; shared loci, names, taxa or citations do not transfer identity, production, activity or mechanism.
- Two original undefined-stereo and two charge-rearrangement warnings remain global and unassigned. No additional molecular/YAML parse, source query or target invocation occurred during scientific authoring.
- No source record or native status/history is edited. Computed model concerns do not automatically establish a maintained-source defect.
- The whole selected owner was read, but scientific completion is partial because full QC, complete ontology authority coverage and external source verification were unavailable. No native status or history was changed.
- The branch uses cached base ce6a5498df5be51116bd5e0c119023ee62bb5464. Fresh origin/main verification and publication are deferred because the user prohibits all external requests.
- Support personally read all five complete raw owners/history, actual intakes and the disjoint source partition; exact prior companion carries and BGC/Native complementary readings remain attributed in the joint notes.
- Support personally read all ten saved forms: 312 atom and 338 bond rows, every scalar/ring/stereo/motif/map (a98425, da0f1d, d898a7, dc945f). The original correspondence limits are preserved; no new graph computation or molecular parse.
- ROOT operated activation/capture, inventory/composer, canonical intakes and the diagnostic. Native operated original gates and maintained reader under ROOT authorization; ROOT separately accepted results. Scientific authoring invoked none of these targets.
- Saved review coverage is distinct from the native SEEDED/REVIEWED curation state. Proposed actions are not performed edits.

- assessment_id: support-2348-representation
  area: representation
  topic: Representation
  outcome: supported
  summary: Both complete forms are neutral C20H32O2 with 22 heavy atoms, 24 bonds
    and one component. They retain a bridged carbocyclic core containing a three-membered
    ring, a side-chain ketone, a tertiary hydroxyl and an alkene with identical methyl
    substituents at one end, so that alkene has no E/Z requirement. One tetrahedral
    centre is specified R and five potential tetrahedral centres remain unspecified,
    matching stereo_complete:false. The saved ring lists contain alternative cycles
    rather than four independent rings. All ten original comparison flags are true,
    and the recorded 22-atom correspondence remains explicitly short of independent
    graph/natural-identity proof. No parser, structure cleanup or experimental stereo
    assignment was added.

- assessment_id: support-2348-classification
  area: consistency
  topic: Classification
  outcome: concern
  summary: NPClassifier npclassifier.gnps2.org@2026-09-07 faithfully retains Terpenoids,
    Sesquiterpenoids, Santalane sesquiterpenoids and is_glycoside:false. The complete
    C20 oxygenated representation and the retained C20 precursor narrative make the
    sesquiterpenoid label an applicability concern, while the santalane-like core
    alone does not settle whole-molecule biosynthetic classification. No wrong source
    identity or directory change follows automatically from a faithful model prediction.
    The full tables contain no sugar residue or O-carbon-O linkage that would contradict
    the false glycoside output.

- assessment_id: support-2348-pathway
  area: graph
  topic: Pathway
  outcome: supported
  summary: The complete raw owner declares two BIOSYNTHESIS graphs and no biosynthetic_pathway
    root. The CPT2 assay/transgenic graph has eleven nodes and ten individually supported
    edges; the transcript-context graph has ten nodes and twelve edges. The first
    separates precursor condensation from engineered-tissue product detection. Recombinant
    CPT2 lacking 51 residues was assayed with IPP and Z,Z-FPP using a hydrolyzed radiolabel
    readout assigned by the source to NNPP, not direct final-product catalysis or
    independent precursor structural proof. The selected three 35S MP1 plants and
    transgenic petiolule comparisons retain source assignment, bounded wild-type nondetection
    and the unresolved S2 peak4 dominant-ion difference between chromatogramD and
    B/C/standardE. The second graph preserves normalized qRT-PCR tissue/fraction patterns,
    not single-cell localization, coordinated protein abundance, direct regulation,
    physical assembly or reactions imported from incompletely inspected earlier papers.
    The accepted original maintained result preserves the complete owner, both graphs,
    their ordered evidence and all four history events; the biosynthetic_pathway root
    remains absent. This establishes preservation, not independent biochemical verification.
  target_ids:
  - naturalproductmech:mibig-7163f2db37
  evidence_ids:
  - support-2348-mechanism-owner
  - support-2348-reader
  - support-2348-reader-acceptance
  - support-2348-reader-execution
- assessment_id: support-2348-protein
  area: graph
  topic: Protein
  outcome: supported
  summary: K7WQ45, R9R6P6 and K4CI56 are deliberate current native-sequence examples
    for CPT2, TPS21 and CYP71BN1, not proven historical assay stocks or MP1 alleles.
    The CPT2 preparation and 35S construct remain REVIEW_NEEDED and ungrounded; the
    51-residue deletion differs from the 1–61 predicted transit region and does not
    establish mature processing or tag handling. The retained CPT2 CDS comparisons
    and all three S1 primer matches were bounded to the eleven annotated KC807995.1
    CDSs, not genome-wide specificity. K4CI56 retains its Heinz1706 source and generic
    P450 scope. Transcript support does not demonstrate purified TPS21/CYP71BN1 reaction
    chemistry, complexes or direct final-product catalysis. Historical sequence checks
    are carried with their limits and are not refreshed.
  target_ids:
  - naturalproductmech:mibig-7163f2db37
  evidence_ids:
  - support-2348-mechanism-owner
  - support-2348-reader
  - support-2348-reader-acceptance
  - support-2348-reader-execution
